ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-19-sejm-84-3.u546	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Civic Platform - Civic Coalition Club, I will present my position towards the Senate draft law amending the Family and Care Code Act and the Government bill amending the Family and Care Code Act. Both projects shall carry out the judgment of the Constitutional Court of 16 May 2018 in which the Tribunal has ruled that Article. 70 § I of the Code, in so far as it sets a time limit for an action for denial of the fatherhood of the mother's husband, regardless of the date on which an adult child hears about the fact that it does not come from a mother's husband, is incompatible with the Constitution. The claim for denial of fatherhood is the only mechanism laid down by law to overturn the presumption of legal origin from the mother's husband. The current solution, which is to close the investigation of the denial of fatherhood after the expiry of the 3-year deadline, is undoubtedly a restriction on the constitutional right to know its own biological roots. It is not a problem in this case that, with the expiry of 3 years from the date on which the child reaches adulthood, the possibility of challenging the legal presumption of the article is definitely lost. 62 § 1 or 2 k.r.o., but the fact that this term is completely detached from the moment when the person concerned learned that it was not from his mother's husband. Therefore, the projects propose to modify the time limit for the denial of both fatherhood and motherhood, as well as, in the government project, to determine the inefficiencies of recognition. The Senate project envisages that this deadline will be 3 years from the date of age, except that when a child becomes aware of this situation after he or she is of age, the time limit starts to run from the date on which the child becomes aware of the circumstances. On the other hand, the government project assumes that it will be possible to bring an action within a year from the date on which the child learned of this circumstance. In addition, the government project envisages changing the beginning of deadlines for mother and mother's husband to bring an action for denial of fatherhood. They would start from the day these people learn that the child does not come from the mother's husband. High Izbo! Given that the projects are the execution of the Constitutional Court's judgment and the growing importance of the possibility of establishing origin on the basis of biological truth, we are in favour of directing both projects to further work in order to find final solutions.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-19-sejm-84-3.u547	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour on behalf of the club Polskie Stronnictwo Ludowe - Coalition Polska to present our position on the government draft law amending the Family and Care Code Act (print No 3573) and the Senate draft law amending the Family and Care Code Act (print No 3486). The draft amendment of the Family and Care Code, contained in print No 3573, implies a modification of the beginning of the period for the child to bring an action for denial of motherhood, denial of fatherhood and determination of the inefficiencies of the recognition of fatherhood. If a child learns of a lack of biological origin from a person whose motherhood or fatherhood has been established - before he reaches his age - the time limit for bringing an action for denial of motherhood, denial of fatherhood and determination of the inefficiencies of recognition of fatherhood will count from the date on which they reach adulthood. The child will have a year to bring such an action. The proposal also proposes changing the beginning of deadlines for mother and mother's husband to bring an action for denial of fatherhood. He would start running from the day these people learned that the child did not come from his mother's husband. At the same time, there will be a restriction that the bringing of an action may take place at the latest by the date on which the child reaches age. High Izbo! The annual time limits contained in the project should be considered appropriate and appropriate. On the one hand, they offer an opportunity to make a thoughtful decision with extremely serious and long-term consequences, and on the other hand, they do not unduly destabilise the legal situation of the child. The second Senate project (print No 3486) is the implementation of the judgment of the Constitutional Tribunal of 16 May 2018. The Court held that Article. 70 § I k.r.o. to the extent that it sets a time limit for an action to be brought against the fatherhood of the mother's husband, regardless of the date on which an adult child becomes aware of the fact that it does not originate from his mother's husband, it is incompatible with the constitution. The contested article states that a child may bring an action for denial of the fatherhood of his mother no later than 3 years after his or her age. This poses a difficult situation for those who have learned about their real biological father later than within the three years after they have completed their teenage years. Such persons do not have the opportunity to bring an action for denial of paternity, which violates their constitutional right to know and legal recognition of their origin in accordance with biological truth. In view of the above, the authors of the senate project propose that the 3-year term should be counted against the attainment of the child's age, unless the difference between the legally established branch bond and the biological bond of the child is known only after the child's age is reached. In such a situation, the date on which the person concerned becomes aware of the circumstances on which the action is based will be valid for the start of the period. High Izbo! The project corrects the obvious lack in the Family and Care Code, which has so far prevented people from changing their legal status until they were 21 years old who found out who their biological father was. The proposal enabling such persons to take appropriate legal action in accordance with the Constitutional Tribunal's judgment raises no doubt.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-19-sejm-84-3.u548	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Polish Free and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the Government's draft law amending the Family and Care Code Act (Print No 3573) and the Senate's draft law amending the Family and Care Code Act (Print No 3486). Both of these projects aim to bring the Polish system of right into line with the judgment of the Constitutional Tribunal of 16 May 2018, in which the non-compliance of the article was found. 70 § 1 of the Law of 25 February 1964. The Family and Care Code with the Constitution. According to the contested rules, a child may bring an action after reaching adulthood to deny the fatherhood of his mother's husband, but no later than three years after his or her age was reached. The Senators of the Republic of Poland propose that a three-year period for the child to bring an action for denial of fatherhood should be counted from the date on which they took the news of their origin. The government project is a further project. In addition to changing the calculation of the start of this period, it is also assumed to be reduced to 1 year. In addition, it provides for an extension from 6 months to 1 year of time limits limiting the active card to question the origin of the child. Knowledge of its biological origin is becoming increasingly important not only for emotional reasons, but also for health reasons through the development of medicine. Information about what diseases we are genetically burdened with affects how we live daily and how we use prevention. Therefore, I consider the amendments proposed in both projects to be extremely necessary. I'm happy with the government's initiative. The Committee welcomes the fact that, when designing the changes discussed today, the experts did not merely limit themselves to the regulations challenged by the Constitutional Tribunal, but looked at the problem holistically. Thanks to this, we are talking today about a solution that covers the whole problem, not just its clipping. However, despite the well-founded argument in the explanatory memorandum of the Government's proposal to align the legal situation of all entities directly and not necessarily concerned about the denial of the origin of the child, I have doubts as to whether a reduction to 1 year of the current 3-year period for the child to bring an action is an appropriate compromise. I believe that this may be too far-reaching a limitation compared to today's situation. I trust that this issue will be discussed again in the course of further work. The Free and Solidarni Circle is in favour of directing both projects to further work in the committee.

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-19-sejm-84-3.u550	Thank you, Marshal. On 12 July a beautiful celebration of the Day of Combat and Martyrdom of the Polish Village took place in Szczecin, the commune of Guscieradów, Lubelskie Voivodeship. The Day of Combat and Martyrdom of the Polish Village was established by the present parliament, during the current term of the Sejm, by law of 29 September 2017. The day of 12 July was chosen - as the day on which the greatest pacifications of Polish villages took place, among others. the pacification of the village of Michniów in Kielce by German invaders. This day was established by Parliament as a tribute to the rural population, who experienced oppression, mass murders, displacements, exports to Russian lagoons and German concentration camps, arson of buildings, etc. The martyrdom of the Polish village during the Second World War, which Poles living in rural areas suffered, was a retaliation for fighting in guerrilla troops, for supplying food to occupied cities, for delivering young people to military troops, for giving shelter to guerrillas, for hiding the population displaced from cities all over Poland, and for storing the Jewish population. In such circumstances, hundreds of villages were the victims of the pacification, among others. On February 2, such fate met residents of Borów village in Lublin, near Annopola, where the Germans burned down the church filled with people, then several hundred men, women and children died, burning the whole village at the same time. Individuals survived. Such fate was shared by many Polish villages. Therefore, setting up a day of struggle and martyrdom for them is an expression of homage and memory, and at the same time a warning against the atrocities of war. This year, as I mentioned, the celebrations of the Day of Combat and Martyrdom of the Polish Village took place on 12 July in Szczecyn in the commune of Guscieradów, in the Lublin Voivodeship. The people of this village took part in the celebrations, although it was not a day off from work, and the surrounding villages, representatives of the state authorities together with the Lublin Voivodeship, local self-governments headed by the Marshal of Lublin Voivodeship and parliamentarians, and what is very, very important, school youth and scouts. The celebrations began with Holy Mass. in a local church where flowers were placed in front of the monument in honor of the murdered. The celebrations played a great educational and educational role in the case of Polish youth, as well as an integration role - in the case of villagers. As a participant in the ceremony, I would like to highlight the appealing hospitality of the villagers Szczecin, including the participation, of course, of the village hostels' circles, as well as the support of the local parish priest. During the meeting there was an opportunity to discuss, share experiences, which, I am convinced, will result in new initiatives. Thank you for your attention.

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-19-sejm-84-3.u552	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I wanted to raise the issue of the usefulness of genetic tests in diagnostics and cancer treatment. The cause of cancer is not entirely known, but it is known that genetic factors play a very important role. These may be congenital or acquired factors. However, this is not what I wanted to talk about, how this is happening, but what practically this diagnosis looks like in Poland. In Poland, access to genetic research is very difficult, especially with regard to the population of people who are over 18 years of age. In fact, every cancer patient should have a thorough genetic examination of the cancer he is suffering from and, in addition, a disposition to have cancer. The number of tumor diseases is increasing exponentially. It is estimated that next year it will be 180 thousand. The disease, but I've also encountered reports that we might reach 200,000. The number of cases is increasing for both adults and children. Why is genetics so important? Well, the whole world is now moving towards personalized treatment, which is tailored to the individual patient, depending on what genes the patient has, what genes he inherited from his parents, and what genes the patient has in relation to cancer therapy, e.g. chemotherapy, where some genes cause certain cytostatics to be pumped out of the cancer cell, and thus inefficient. The ideal, and probably future therapy, will be genetics, and in fact genetic cancer treatment, so-called. gene therapy. At the Wrocław clinic, one of the patients with non-Hodgkin's malignant lymphoma, who had a fourth relapse, was saved after two autotransplants. Gene therapy, anti-Bcl-2 anti-senses, which inhibited the Bcl-2 gene, was the cause of the disease, which was resistant to the first, second, third and fourth lines of treatment. The patient who received this treatment in Wrocław was a sick child who was 11 years old at the time, that was in 2001. Both chemotherapy and double megachemia were ineffective, because this is the goal of stem cell administration and autografting. Sometimes I am terrified to hear that innovation is expensive, because such therapy is expensive, such a diagnosis is expensive, but human life is priceless. [[Priceless.]] In view of the above, there must be no shortage of money in such a situation. Each tumor requires a thorough diagnosis, and if there is gene therapy somewhere in the world, because many centers already run it, in various cancers, the Polish patient must be able, if all conventional treatments are used, to receive this form. Thank you for your attention.
